# 数据结构与字段

本页根据随站点发布的 OpenAPI 生成。可选不表示允许 null；只有类型明确包含 null 时才能传 null。嵌套必填列指该字段所在对象，父对象可选时只在提供父对象后校验。

## 工具与 UI 的 Schema 方言

Schema 支持 type、properties、required、additionalProperties:false、items、enum、minimum/maximum、minLength/maxLength、minItems/maxItems、description/title。对象关闭额外字段，最大递归 12 层；默认字符串上限 65536、数组上限 1000。不支持 $ref、pattern、format、oneOf 或远端解析。这里指工具输入输出和 UI Props 的业务 Schema，不是 OpenAPI 文件自身的 Schema 特性。

## Ref {#schema-ref}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | pattern: "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$" |
| version | string | 是 | pattern: "^(0\|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0\|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0\|[1-9][0-9]*)$" |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$"
    },
    "version": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "version"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## Application {#schema-application}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | — |
| name | string | 是 | — |
| revision | integer | 是 | — |
| status | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["DRAFT","ACTIVE","SUSPENDED","ARCHIVED"] |
| defaultAgent | [Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) / null | 是 | — |
| statusReason | string | 否 | maxLength: 512 |
| permissionProvider | [BusinessProvider](./数据结构.md#schema-businessprovider) | 否 | — |
| contextProvider | [ContextProvider](./数据结构.md#schema-contextprovider) | 否 | — |
| securityEpoch | integer | 否 | minimum: 0 |
| evaluationGate | [EvaluationGate](./数据结构.md#schema-evaluationgate) | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "revision": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "status": {
      "enum": [
        "DRAFT",
        "ACTIVE",
        "SUSPENDED",
        "ARCHIVED"
      ]
    },
    "defaultAgent": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "statusReason": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 512
    },
    "permissionProvider": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/BusinessProvider"
    },
    "contextProvider": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ContextProvider"
    },
    "securityEpoch": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "evaluationGate": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/EvaluationGate"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "name",
    "revision",
    "status",
    "defaultAgent"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## CreateApplication {#schema-createapplication}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | — |
| name | string | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## CreateToken {#schema-createtoken}

subjectId 非空；tenantId 默认为空字符串。原始 token 仅签发时返回。

| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| subjectId | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128 |
| tenantId | string | 否 | maxLength: 128 |
| role | 枚举 | 否 | default: "USER"；enum: ["USER","APP_ADMIN"] |
| ttlSeconds | integer | 否 | default: 86400；minimum: 60；maximum: 2592000 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "subjectId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "role": {
      "enum": [
        "USER",
        "APP_ADMIN"
      ],
      "default": "USER"
    },
    "ttlSeconds": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 60,
      "maximum": 2592000,
      "default": 86400
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "subjectId"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

```json
      "type": "string"
    },
    "version": {

    }
```

## Token {#schema-token}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | — |
| appId | string | 是 | — |
| subjectId | string | 是 | — |
| tenantId | string | 是 | — |
| role | string | 是 | — |
| expiresAt | integer | 是 | format: "int64" |
| revoked | boolean | 是 | — |
| token | string | 否 | Only returned once by POST tokens |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "appId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "subjectId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "role": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "expiresAt": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64"
    },
    "revoked": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "token": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Only returned once by POST tokens"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "appId",
    "subjectId",
    "tenantId",
    "role",
    "expiresAt",
    "revoked"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## Definition {#schema-definition}

根据 Registry kind 选择对应定义；不把不同类别的字段混合提交。

类型：[AgentDefinition](./数据结构.md#schema-agentdefinition) / [ToolDefinition](./数据结构.md#schema-tooldefinition) / [KnowledgeDefinition](./数据结构.md#schema-knowledgedefinition) / [UiDefinition](./数据结构.md#schema-uidefinition) / [ProviderDefinition](./数据结构.md#schema-providerdefinition) / [ModelDefinition](./数据结构.md#schema-modeldefinition) / [BindingDefinition](./数据结构.md#schema-bindingdefinition) / [ProfileDefinition](./数据结构.md#schema-profiledefinition)。—

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "oneOf": [
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentDefinition"
    },
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ToolDefinition"
    },
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/KnowledgeDefinition"
    },
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/UiDefinition"
    },
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ProviderDefinition"
    },
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ModelDefinition"
    },
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/BindingDefinition"
    },
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ProfileDefinition"
    }
  ]
}
```

:::

| version | string | 是 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "version": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "kind": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "appId": {
      "type": "string"
    },

        "REVOKED"
      ]

    }

    "appId",
    "revision",
    "status",
    "definition"
```

## AgentDefinition {#schema-agentdefinition}

允许列表默认空；delegatedCapabilities 采用 kind/id@version。根 Agent 与子 Agent 共用根任务预算。

| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| systemPrompt | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 32768 |
| modelProfile | [Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 是 | — |
| allowedTools | 数组：[Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 否 | default: [] |
| allowedKnowledge | 数组：[Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 否 | default: [] |
| allowedAgents | 数组：[Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 否 | default: [] |
| allowedUi | 数组：[Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 否 | default: [] |
| delegatedCapabilities | 数组：string | 否 | kind/id@version for all delegated capabilities, including descendant ceilings |
| limits | object | 否 | 不允许额外字段；根调用汇总全树；子调用按自己的定义预留父级剩余额度，结束归还未用容量。供应商 Token 与费用在网络发送前原子预留。 |
| limits.maxSteps | integer | 否 | default: 8；minimum: 1；maximum: 32 |
| limits.maxToolCalls | integer | 否 | default: 16；minimum: 0；maximum: 64 |
| limits.maxDepth | integer | 否 | default: 3；minimum: 0；maximum: 8 |
| limits.timeoutSeconds | integer | 否 | default: 120；minimum: 1；maximum: 600 |
| limits.maxTokens | integer | 否 | default: 32000；minimum: 256；maximum: 1000000；字符预算的旧字段；与 maxBudgetUnits 不能同时设置。 |
| limits.maxAgentCalls | integer | 否 | default: 8；minimum: 0；maximum: 64 |
| limits.maxBudgetUnits | integer | 否 | default: 32000；minimum: 256；maximum: 1000000 |
| limits.maxProviderTokens | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000000000 |
| limits.maxCost | object | 否 | 不允许额外字段 |
| limits.maxCost.currency | string | 是 | pattern: "^[A-Z]{3}$" |
| limits.maxCost.amount | string | 是 | pattern: "^[0-9]{1,13}(\\.[0-9]{1,12})?$" |
| entryPolicy | object | 否 | 不允许额外字段；Direct chat entry authorization. Omitted means APPLICATION. Delegation is separately authorized by allowedAgents. RESTRICTED filters are combined with AND; values inside each filter use OR. |
| entryPolicy.mode | string | 否 | default: "APPLICATION"；enum: ["APPLICATION","RESTRICTED","INTERNAL_ONLY"] |
| entryPolicy.roles | 数组：string | 否 | maxItems: 256；uniqueItems: true |
| entryPolicy.subjects | 数组：string | 否 | maxItems: 256；uniqueItems: true |
| entryPolicy.tenants | 数组：string | 否 | maxItems: 256；uniqueItems: true |
| permissions | 数组：string | 否 | maxItems: 64；uniqueItems: true；业务权限名称；需认证权限 Provider，未配置则拒绝受保护能力。 |
| requiredCapabilities | 数组：string | 否 | maxItems: 5；uniqueItems: true |
| allowedModelRegions | 数组：string | 否 | minItems: 1；maxItems: 32；uniqueItems: true |
| knowledgeRouting | [KnowledgeRoutingPolicy](./数据结构.md#schema-knowledgeroutingpolicy) | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "systemPrompt": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 32768
    },
    "modelProfile": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
    },
    "allowedTools": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
      },
      "default": []
    },
    "allowedKnowledge": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
      },
      "default": []
    },
    "allowedAgents": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
      },
      "default": []
    },
    "allowedUi": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
      },
      "default": []
    },
    "delegatedCapabilities": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      },
      "description": "kind/id@version for all delegated capabilities, including descendant ceilings"
    },
    "limits": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "maxSteps": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 1,
          "maximum": 32,
          "default": 8
        },
        "maxToolCalls": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 64,
          "default": 16
        },
        "maxDepth": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 8,
          "default": 3
        },
        "timeoutSeconds": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 1,
          "maximum": 600,
          "default": 120
        },
        "maxTokens": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 256,
          "maximum": 1000000,
          "default": 32000,
          "deprecated": true,
          "description": "字符预算的旧字段；与 maxBudgetUnits 不能同时设置。"
        },
        "maxAgentCalls": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 64,
          "default": 8
        },
        "maxBudgetUnits": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 256,
          "maximum": 1000000,
          "default": 32000
        },
        "maxProviderTokens": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 1000000000000
        },
        "maxCost": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "currency": {
              "type": "string",
              "pattern": "^[A-Z]{3}$"
            },
            "amount": {
              "type": "string",
              "pattern": "^[0-9]{1,13}(\\.[0-9]{1,12})?$"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "currency",
            "amount"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      },
      "required": [],
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "not": {
        "required": [
          "maxTokens",
          "maxBudgetUnits"
        ]
      },
      "description": "根调用汇总全树；子调用按自己的定义预留父级剩余额度，结束归还未用容量。供应商 Token 与费用在网络发送前原子预留。"
    },
    "entryPolicy": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "Direct chat entry authorization. Omitted means APPLICATION. Delegation is separately authorized by allowedAgents. RESTRICTED filters are combined with AND; values inside each filter use OR.",
      "properties": {
        "mode": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "APPLICATION",
            "RESTRICTED",
            "INTERNAL_ONLY"
          ],
          "default": "APPLICATION"
        },
        "roles": {
          "type": "array",
          "maxItems": 256,
          "uniqueItems": true,
          "items": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 128
          }
        },
        "subjects": {
          "type": "array",
          "maxItems": 256,
          "uniqueItems": true,
          "items": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 128
          }
        },
        "tenants": {
          "type": "array",
          "maxItems": 256,
          "uniqueItems": true,
          "items": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 128
          }
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "permissions": {
      "type": "array",
      "maxItems": 64,
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{0,127}$"
      },
      "description": "业务权限名称；需认证权限 Provider，未配置则拒绝受保护能力。"
    },
    "requiredCapabilities": {
      "type": "array",
      "maxItems": 5,
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "enum": [
          "toolCalling",
          "structuredOutput",
          "vision",
          "audio",
          "streaming"
        ]
      }
    },
    "allowedModelRegions": {
      "type": "array",
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 32,
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$"
      }
    },
    "knowledgeRouting": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/KnowledgeRoutingPolicy"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "systemPrompt",
    "modelProfile"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## ToolDefinition {#schema-tooldefinition}

roles 必须为非空 USER/APP_ADMIN 数组；effect 为 READ、COMPUTE、WRITE、DANGEROUS。后者要求用户确认。

| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| description | string | 否 | — |
| endpoint | string | 是 | format: "uri" |
| secretRef | string | 否 | — |
| effect | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["READ","COMPUTE","WRITE","DANGEROUS"] |
| roles | 数组：枚举 | 是 | minItems: 1 |
| inputSchema | object | 是 | — |
| outputSchema | object | 是 | — |
| leaseRequired | boolean | 否 | default: false |
| permissions | 数组：string | 否 | maxItems: 64；uniqueItems: true；业务权限名称；需认证权限 Provider，未配置则拒绝受保护能力。 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "description": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "endpoint": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uri"
    },
    "secretRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "effect": {
      "enum": [
        "READ",
        "COMPUTE",
        "WRITE",
        "DANGEROUS"
      ]
    },
    "roles": {
      "type": "array",
      "minItems": 1,
      "items": {
        "enum": [
          "USER",
          "APP_ADMIN"
        ]
      }
    },
    "inputSchema": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": true
    },
    "outputSchema": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": true
    },
    "leaseRequired": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "permissions": {
      "type": "array",
      "maxItems": 64,
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{0,127}$"
      },
      "description": "业务权限名称；需认证权限 Provider，未配置则拒绝受保护能力。"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "endpoint",
    "effect",
    "roles",
    "inputSchema",
    "outputSchema"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## KnowledgeDefinition {#schema-knowledgedefinition}

INLINE 要求 1–100 篇 documents，每篇 text 1–16384 字符；REMOTE 必须有 endpoint；MANAGED 通过独立文档接口导入。

| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| description | string | 否 | — |
| mode | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["INLINE","REMOTE","MANAGED"] |
| endpoint | string | 否 | format: "uri" |
| secretRef | string | 否 | — |
| topK | integer | 否 | default: 3；minimum: 1；maximum: 10 |
| documents | 数组：object | 否 | minItems: 1；maxItems: 100 |
| documents[].id | string | 是 | — |
| documents[].text | string | 是 | — |
| documents[].title | string | 否 | — |
| documents[].source | string | 否 | — |
| documents[].tenantId | string | 否 | — |
| leaseRequired | boolean | 否 | default: false |
| permissions | 数组：string | 否 | maxItems: 64；uniqueItems: true；业务权限名称；需认证权限 Provider，未配置则拒绝受保护能力。 |
| callbackProtocolVersion | string | 否 | const: "1.0"；仅 REMOTE；缺省沿用旧 documents 合同。 |
| mandatoryFilters | 数组：枚举 | 否 | maxItems: 2；uniqueItems: true |
| tags | 数组：string | 否 | maxItems: 16；uniqueItems: true |
| chunking | [DocumentChunkingPolicy](./数据结构.md#schema-documentchunkingpolicy) | 否 | — |
| retrieval | [KnowledgeRetrieval](./数据结构.md#schema-knowledgeretrieval) | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "description": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "mode": {
      "enum": [
        "INLINE",
        "REMOTE",
        "MANAGED"
      ]
    },
    "endpoint": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uri"
    },
    "secretRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "topK": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 10,
      "default": 3
    },
    "documents": {
      "type": "array",
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 100,
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "text": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "title": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "source": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "tenantId": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "id",
          "text"
        ],
        "additionalProperties": false
      }
    },
    "leaseRequired": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "permissions": {
      "type": "array",
      "maxItems": 64,
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{0,127}$"
      },
      "description": "业务权限名称；需认证权限 Provider，未配置则拒绝受保护能力。"
    },
    "callbackProtocolVersion": {
      "const": "1.0",
      "description": "仅 REMOTE；缺省沿用旧 documents 合同。"
    },
    "mandatoryFilters": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "enum": [
          "TENANT",
          "SUBJECT"
        ]
      },
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "maxItems": 2
    },
    "tags": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 64
      },
      "maxItems": 16,
      "uniqueItems": true
    },
    "chunking": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/DocumentChunkingPolicy"
    },
    "retrieval": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/KnowledgeRetrieval"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "mode"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "allOf": [
    {
      "if": {
        "required": [
          "callbackProtocolVersion"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "mode": {
            "const": "REMOTE"
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "required": [
          "mandatoryFilters"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "required": [
          "callbackProtocolVersion"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "callbackProtocolVersion": {
            "const": "1.0"
          },
          "mode": {
            "const": "REMOTE"
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "callbackProtocolVersion": {
            "const": "1.0"
          },
          "permissions": {
            "minItems": 1
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "callbackProtocolVersion",
          "permissions"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "required": [
          "mandatoryFilters"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "mandatoryFilters": {
            "contains": {
              "const": "SUBJECT"
            }
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "required": [
          "chunking"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "mode": {
            "const": "MANAGED"
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "required": [
          "retrieval"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "mode": {
            "const": "MANAGED"
          }
        }
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

:::

## UiDefinition {#schema-uidefinition}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| description | string | 否 | — |
| propsSchema | object | 是 | — |
| fallback | string | 是 | minLength: 1 |
| permissions | 数组：string | 否 | maxItems: 64；uniqueItems: true；业务权限名称；需认证权限 Provider，未配置则拒绝受保护能力。 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "description": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "propsSchema": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": true
    },
    "fallback": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1
    },
    "permissions": {
      "type": "array",
      "maxItems": 64,
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{0,127}$"
      },
      "description": "业务权限名称；需认证权限 Provider，未配置则拒绝受保护能力。"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "propsSchema",
    "fallback"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## ProviderDefinition {#schema-providerdefinition}

stream=true 可用于 OPENAI_CHAT 与 REMOTE 1.0；REMOTE 事件、身份及终结要求见 RemoteModelRequest/RemoteModelStreamFrame。endpoint 为完整请求地址，受出站白名单和密钥 origin 绑定限制。

| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| mode | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["OPENAI_CHAT","REMOTE"] |
| endpoint | string | 是 | format: "uri" |
| secretRef | string | 否 | — |
| stream | boolean | 否 | default: false；OPENAI_CHAT 为原生 SSE；REMOTE 为版本 1.0 的统一模型 SSE。stream 默认 false，模型必须显式声明 streaming=true。 |
| leaseRequired | boolean | 否 | default: false |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "mode": {
      "enum": [
        "OPENAI_CHAT",
        "REMOTE"
      ]
    },
    "endpoint": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uri"
    },
    "secretRef": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "stream": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "OPENAI_CHAT 为原生 SSE；REMOTE 为版本 1.0 的统一模型 SSE。stream 默认 false，模型必须显式声明 streaming=true。"
    },
    "leaseRequired": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "mode",
    "endpoint"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## ModelDefinition {#schema-modeldefinition}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| provider | [Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 是 | — |
| modelId | string | 是 | — |
| pricing | object | 否 | 不允许额外字段 |
| pricing.currency | string | 是 | pattern: "^[A-Z]{3}$" |
| pricing.inputPerMillion | number | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000 |
| pricing.outputPerMillion | number | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000 |
| capabilities | [ModelCapabilities](./数据结构.md#schema-modelcapabilities) | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "provider": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
    },
    "modelId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "pricing": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "currency": {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^[A-Z]{3}$"
        },
        "inputPerMillion": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 1000000
        },
        "outputPerMillion": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 1000000
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "currency",
        "inputPerMillion",
        "outputPerMillion"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "capabilities": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ModelCapabilities"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "provider",
    "modelId"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## BindingDefinition {#schema-bindingdefinition}

scope 省略表示当前应用，也可明确填写当前 appId；global 绑定仅平台管理员授权。

| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| model | [Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 是 | — |
| scope | string | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "model": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
    },
    "scope": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "model"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## ProfileDefinition {#schema-profiledefinition}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| binding | [Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 是 | — |
| maxOutputTokens | integer | 否 | default: 1024；minimum: 1；maximum: 32768 |
| outputSchema | object | 否 | 平台闭合 Schema 方言；根为 object，嵌套对象全部属性 required，完整输出验证后才发送文字。 |
| fallbackBindings | 数组：[Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 否 | maxItems: 7；uniqueItems: true；固定候选绑定版本；与主绑定不能重复，全部候选必须符合智能体兼容性。 |
| routing | [ModelRoutingPolicy](./数据结构.md#schema-modelroutingpolicy) | 否 | — |
| parameters | [ModelGenerationParameters](./数据结构.md#schema-modelgenerationparameters) | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "binding": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
    },
    "maxOutputTokens": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 32768,
      "default": 1024
    },
    "outputSchema": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "平台闭合 Schema 方言；根为 object，嵌套对象全部属性 required，完整输出验证后才发送文字。",
      "additionalProperties": true
    },
    "fallbackBindings": {
      "type": "array",
      "maxItems": 7,
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
      },
      "description": "固定候选绑定版本；与主绑定不能重复，全部候选必须符合智能体兼容性。"
    },
    "routing": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ModelRoutingPolicy"
    },
    "parameters": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ModelGenerationParameters"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "binding"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## ChatRequest {#schema-chatrequest}

agentId 非空时必须同时提供 agentVersion；appId 若提供须等于路径。conversationId 省略/null 创建会话；context 与 client 不是可信身份。

| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| protocolVersion | string | 是 | const: "1.0" |
| appId | string | 否 | — |
| agentId | string / null | 否 | — |
| agentVersion | string | 否 | — |
| conversationId | string / null | 否 | — |
| message | object | 是 | 不允许额外字段 |
| message.role | string | 是 | const: "user" |
| message.content | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 16384 |
| context | object | 否 | — |
| client | object | 否 | 不允许额外字段 |
| client.platform | string | 否 | — |
| client.uiCapabilities | 数组：string | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "protocolVersion": {
      "const": "1.0"
    },
    "appId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "agentId": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "agentVersion": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "conversationId": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "message": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "role": {
          "const": "user"
        },
        "content": {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 16384
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "role",
        "content"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "context": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": true
    },
    "client": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "platform": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "uiCapabilities": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [],
      "additionalProperties": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "protocolVersion",
    "message"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |

```json
        "approve",

      ]
```

## Run {#schema-run}

202 返回的 Run 尚未完成；终态为 SUCCEEDED、FAILED、CANCELLED、TIMED_OUT。

| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | — |
| appId | string | 是 | — |
| subjectId | string | 否 | — |
| tenantId | string | 否 | — |
| status | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["QUEUED","RUNNING","WAITING_CONFIRMATION","SUCCEEDED","FAILED","CANCELLED","TIMED_OUT"] |
| lastSequence | integer | 是 | — |
| traceId | string | 是 | — |
| conversationId | string | 是 | — |
| messageId | string | 是 | — |
| createdAt | integer | 否 | — |
| deadline | integer | 否 | — |
| finishedAt | integer | 否 | — |
| errorCode | string / null | 否 | — |
| agent | [Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 否 | — |
| ownerId | string | 否 | — |
| archived | boolean | 否 | — |
| usage | object | 否 | — |
| knowledgeAccessEpoch | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；Application knowledge permission generation; previous runs/conversations become unavailable after a policy change |
| securityEpoch | integer | 否 | minimum: 0 |
| modelLedger | boolean | 否 | — |
| modelAccounting | [ModelAccountingSummary](./数据结构.md#schema-modelaccountingsummary) | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "required": [
    "id",
    "appId",
    "status",
    "lastSequence",
    "traceId",
    "conversationId",
    "messageId"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "appId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "subjectId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "status": {
      "enum": [
        "QUEUED",
        "RUNNING",
        "WAITING_CONFIRMATION",
        "SUCCEEDED",
        "FAILED",
        "CANCELLED",
        "TIMED_OUT"
      ]
    },
    "lastSequence": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "traceId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "conversationId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "messageId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "createdAt": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "deadline": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "finishedAt": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "errorCode": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "agent": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
    },
    "ownerId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "archived": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "usage": {
      "type": "object"
    },
    "knowledgeAccessEpoch": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "description": "Application knowledge permission generation; previous runs/conversations become unavailable after a policy change"
    },
    "securityEpoch": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "modelLedger": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "modelAccounting": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ModelAccountingSummary"
    }
  }
}
```

:::

| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |

```json
      "properties": {
        "code": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "message": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "traceId": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "retryable": {
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "details": {
          "type": "object",
          "additionalProperties": true
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "code",
        "message",
        "traceId",
        "retryable",
        "details"
      ],
```

## AgentEvent {#schema-agentevent}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| protocolVersion | string | 是 | const: "1.0" |
| event | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["message.started","status.changed","text.delta","agent.started","agent.completed","tool.started","tool.completed","knowledge.retrieved","citation.created","ui.created","confirmation.required","usage.updated","error","message.completed","model.started","model.completed","knowledge.planned","knowledge.fused","citation.verified"] |
| runId | string | 是 | — |
| traceId | string | 是 | — |
| conversationId | string | 是 | — |
| messageId | string | 是 | — |
| sequence | integer | 是 | minimum: 1 |
| data | object | 是 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "title": "SparkTide AgentEvent 1.0",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "protocolVersion": {
      "const": "1.0"
    },
    "event": {
      "enum": [
        "message.started",
        "status.changed",
        "text.delta",
        "agent.started",
        "agent.completed",
        "tool.started",
        "tool.completed",
        "knowledge.retrieved",
        "citation.created",
        "ui.created",
        "confirmation.required",
        "usage.updated",
        "error",
        "message.completed",
        "model.started",
        "model.completed",
        "knowledge.planned",
        "knowledge.fused",
        "citation.verified"
      ]
    },
    "runId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "traceId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "conversationId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "messageId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "sequence": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "data": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": true
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "protocolVersion",
    "event",
    "runId",
    "traceId",
    "conversationId",
    "messageId",
    "sequence",
    "data"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "allOf": [
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "event": {
            "const": "message.started"
          }
        }
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "data": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "status": {
                "type": "string"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "status"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": true
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "event": {
            "const": "status.changed"
          }
        }
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "data": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "status": {
                "type": "string"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "status"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": true
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "event": {
            "const": "text.delta"
          }
        }
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "data": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "text": {
                "type": "string"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "text"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": true
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "event": {
            "const": "agent.started"
          }
        }
      },
      "then": {

                  "$ref": "#/components/schemas/CitationVerification"
                }
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "agentCallId",
              "agentId",
              "agentVersion",
              "parentAgentCallId",
              "depth",
              "references"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": false
          }
        }
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

:::

## DocumentAccess {#schema-documentaccess}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| enabled | boolean | 是 | — |
| subjects | 数组：string | 是 | maxItems: 256；uniqueItems: true |
| tenants | 数组：string | 是 | maxItems: 256；uniqueItems: true |
| roles | 数组：string | 是 | maxItems: 256；uniqueItems: true |
| revision | integer | 是 | minimum: 0 |
| reason | string | 否 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 1024 |
| updatedAt | integer | 否 | — |
| updatedBy | string | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "enabled": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "subjects": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 128
      },
      "maxItems": 256,
      "uniqueItems": true
    },
    "tenants": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 128
      },
      "maxItems": 256,
      "uniqueItems": true
    },

      "type": "array",

      "minimum": 0

      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024
    },

      "type": "integer"
    },
    "updatedBy": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "enabled",
    "subjects",
    "tenants",
    "roles",
    "revision"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## BusinessContextSchema {#schema-businesscontextschema}



类型：object。平台有限 JSON Schema 方言，根必须为 object，所有对象 additionalProperties=false；不能包含身份字段。

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "description": "平台有限 JSON Schema 方言，根必须为 object，所有对象 additionalProperties=false；不能包含身份字段。"
}
```

:::

## BusinessProvider {#schema-businessprovider}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| endpoint | string | 否 | format: "uri" |
| secretRef | string | 否 | minLength: 1 |
| timeoutMs | integer | 否 | default: 2000；minimum: 100；maximum: 10000 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "endpoint": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uri"
    },
    "secretRef": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1
    },
    "timeoutMs": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 100,
      "maximum": 10000,
      "default": 2000
    }
  },
  "description": "空对象停用；非空对象必须有 endpoint 与 secretRef。配置变更需 reason，递增 securityEpoch。",
  "dependentRequired": {
    "endpoint": [
      "secretRef"
    ],
    "secretRef": [
      "endpoint"
    ],
    "timeoutMs": [
      "endpoint",
      "secretRef"
    ]
  }
}
```

:::

## ContextProvider {#schema-contextprovider}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| endpoint | string | 否 | format: "uri" |
| secretRef | string | 否 | minLength: 1 |
| timeoutMs | integer | 否 | default: 2000；minimum: 100；maximum: 10000 |
| schema | [BusinessContextSchema](./数据结构.md#schema-businesscontextschema) | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "endpoint": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uri"
    },
    "secretRef": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1
    },
    "timeoutMs": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 100,
      "maximum": 10000,
      "default": 2000
    },
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/BusinessContextSchema"
    }
  },
  "description": "空对象停用；非空对象必须有 endpoint 与 secretRef。配置变更需 reason，递增 securityEpoch。",
  "dependentRequired": {
    "endpoint": [
      "secretRef",
      "schema"
    ],
    "secretRef": [
      "endpoint",
      "schema"
    ],
    "schema": [
      "endpoint",
      "secretRef"
    ],
    "timeoutMs": [
      "endpoint",
      "secretRef",
      "schema"
    ]
  }
}
```

:::

## BusinessIdentity {#schema-businessidentity}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| protocolVersion | string | 是 | const: "1.0" |
| appId | string | 是 | pattern: "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$" |
| subjectId | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128 |
| tenantId | string | 是 | maxLength: 128 |
| role | string | 是 | enum: ["USER","APP_ADMIN"] |
| runId | string | 是 | maxLength: 128 |
| traceId | string | 是 | maxLength: 128 |
| deadline | integer | 是 | minimum: 1 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "protocolVersion": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "1.0"
    },
    "appId": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$"
    },
    "subjectId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "role": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "USER",
        "APP_ADMIN"
      ]
    },
    "runId": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "traceId": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "deadline": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "protocolVersion",
    "appId",
    "subjectId",
    "tenantId",
    "role",
    "runId",
    "traceId",
    "deadline"
  ],
  "description": "认证回调主体；deadline 为 UTC 毫秒，失效请求拒绝。入口解析或历史读取的 runId/traceId 可能为空。"
}
```

:::

## PermissionCallback {#schema-permissioncallback}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| protocolVersion | string | 是 | const: "1.0" |
| appId | string | 是 | pattern: "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$" |
| subjectId | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128 |
| tenantId | string | 是 | maxLength: 128 |
| role | string | 是 | enum: ["USER","APP_ADMIN","PLATFORM_ADMIN"]；调用方角色；平台管理员经平台令牌访问应用资源时为 PLATFORM_ADMIN |
| runId | string | 是 | maxLength: 128 |
| traceId | string | 是 | maxLength: 128 |
| deadline | integer | 是 | minimum: 1 |
| resource | object | 是 | 不允许额外字段 |
| resource.id | string | 是 | pattern: "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$" |
| resource.version | string | 是 | pattern: "^(0\|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0\|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0\|[1-9][0-9]*)$" |
| resource.kind | string | 是 | enum: ["agents","tools","knowledge","ui-capabilities"] |
| action | string | 是 | enum: ["DISCOVER","EXECUTE","READ_HISTORY"] |
| requiredPermissions | 数组：string | 是 | minItems: 1；maxItems: 64；uniqueItems: true |
| arguments | object | 是 | 调用参数；历史检查保留原执行参数，不可用来覆盖身份。 |
| untrustedContext | object | 是 | 客户端上下文，不能提供授权。 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "protocolVersion": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "1.0"
    },
    "appId": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$"
    },
    "subjectId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "role": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "调用方角色；平台管理员经平台令牌访问应用资源时为 PLATFORM_ADMIN",
      "enum": [
        "USER",
        "APP_ADMIN",
        "PLATFORM_ADMIN"
      ]
    },
    "runId": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "traceId": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "deadline": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "resource": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "properties": {
        "id": {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$"
        },
        "version": {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$"
        },
        "kind": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "agents",
            "tools",
            "knowledge",
            "ui-capabilities"
          ]
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "kind",
        "id",
        "version"
      ]
    },
    "action": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "DISCOVER",
        "EXECUTE",
        "READ_HISTORY"
      ]
    },
    "requiredPermissions": {
      "type": "array",
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 64,
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{0,127}$"
      }
    },
    "arguments": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "调用参数；历史检查保留原执行参数，不可用来覆盖身份。"
    },
    "untrustedContext": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "客户端上下文，不能提供授权。"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "protocolVersion",
    "appId",
    "subjectId",
    "tenantId",
    "role",
    "runId",
    "traceId",
    "deadline",
    "resource",
    "action",
    "requiredPermissions",
    "arguments",
    "untrustedContext"
  ]
}
```

:::

## ReleaseRequest {#schema-releaserequest}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| resources | 数组：[ReleaseResource](./数据结构.md#schema-releaseresource) | 是 | minItems: 1；maxItems: 64；同一 kind/id/version 不能重复；可以包含已发布依赖。 |
| defaultAgent | [Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 否 | — |
| reason | string | 否 | maxLength: 512；发布时必须为非空文本；预检可为空。 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "resources": {
      "type": "array",
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 64,
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ReleaseResource"
      },
      "description": "同一 kind/id/version 不能重复；可以包含已发布依赖。"
    },
    "defaultAgent": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
    },
    "reason": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 512,
      "description": "发布时必须为非空文本；预检可为空。"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "resources"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

```json
        "properties": {

            "type": "string"
          },
          "code": {

          },
          "message": {

          }

        "additionalProperties": false

    "evaluationGate": {

    "planHash",
    "applicationRevision",
    "resources",
    "defaultAgent",
    "dependencyCount",
    "errors"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

```json
    },

      "type": "string"

          "version": {
            "type": "string",
            "pattern": "^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$"
          },
          "revision": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },
          "status": {
            "const": "PUBLISHED"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "kind",
          "id",
          "version",
          "revision",
          "status"
        ],
        "additionalProperties": false
      }
    },
    "previousDefaultAgent": {
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "defaultAgent": {
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "applicationRevision": {
      "type": "integer"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "appId",
    "subjectId",
    "createdAt",
    "reason",
    "planHash",
    "resources",
    "previousDefaultAgent",
    "defaultAgent",
    "applicationRevision"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## ModelCapabilities {#schema-modelcapabilities}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| toolCalling | boolean | 否 | — |
| structuredOutput | boolean | 否 | — |
| vision | boolean | 否 | — |
| audio | boolean | 否 | — |
| streaming | boolean | 否 | — |
| contextWindow | integer | 否 | minimum: 1；maximum: 10000000 |
| maxOutputTokens | integer | 否 | minimum: 1；maximum: 10000000 |
| regions | 数组：string | 否 | minItems: 1；maxItems: 32；uniqueItems: true |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "description": "维护者声明；省略为未知，true 非供应商探测证明。",
  "properties": {
    "toolCalling": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "structuredOutput": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "vision": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "audio": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "streaming": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "contextWindow": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 10000000
    },
    "maxOutputTokens": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 10000000
    },
    "regions": {
      "type": "array",
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 32,
      "uniqueItems": true,

      }
    }
  }
}
```

## ModelCompatibilityReport {#schema-modelcompatibilityreport}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| valid | boolean | 是 | — |
| evidence | string | 是 | const: "DECLARED" |
| contextEstimator | string | 是 | const: "UTF8_BYTES_ESTIMATE" |
| checks | 数组：object | 是 | — |
| checks[].resource | string | 是 | — |
| checks[].model | object | 是 | — |
| checks[].model.scope | string | 是 | — |
| checks[].model.id | string | 是 | — |
| checks[].model.version | string | 是 | — |
| checks[].capabilityStates | object | 是 | — |
| checks[].evidence | string | 是 | const: "DECLARED" |
| checks[].outputTokens | integer | 是 | — |
| checks[].modelLimits | object | 是 | — |
| checks[].modelLimits.contextWindow | integer | 否 | — |
| checks[].modelLimits.maxOutputTokens | integer | 否 | — |
| checks[].regions | 数组：string | 是 | — |
| checks[].binding | [Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 否 | — |
| errors | 数组：object | 是 | — |
| errors[].resource | string | 是 | — |
| errors[].code | string | 是 | — |
| errors[].message | string | 是 | — |
| warnings | 数组：object | 是 | — |
| warnings[].resource | string | 是 | — |
| warnings[].code | string | 是 | — |
| warnings[].message | string | 是 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "required": [
    "valid",
    "evidence",
    "contextEstimator",
    "checks",
    "errors",
    "warnings"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "valid": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "evidence": {
      "const": "DECLARED"
    },
    "contextEstimator": {
      "const": "UTF8_BYTES_ESTIMATE"
    },
    "checks": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "required": [
          "resource",
          "model",
          "capabilityStates",
          "evidence",
          "outputTokens",
          "modelLimits",
          "regions"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "resource": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "model": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "scope": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "id": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "version": {
                "type": "string"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "scope",
              "id",
              "version"
            ]
          },
          "capabilityStates": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": {
              "enum": [
                "DECLARED_SUPPORTED",
                "UNKNOWN",
                "UNSUPPORTED"
              ]
            }
          },
          "evidence": {
            "const": "DECLARED"
          },
          "outputTokens": {
            "type": "integer"
          },
          "modelLimits": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "contextWindow": {
                "type": "integer"
              },
              "maxOutputTokens": {
                "type": "integer"
              }
            }
          },
          "regions": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "binding": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
          }
        }
      }
    },
    "errors": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "required": [
          "resource",
          "code",
          "message"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "resource": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "code": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "message": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        }
      }
    },
    "warnings": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "required": [
          "resource",
          "code",
          "message"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "resource": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "code": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "message": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

:::

## ModelRoutingPolicy {#schema-modelroutingpolicy}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| strategy | 枚举 | 否 | default: "ORDERED"；enum: ["ORDERED","LOWEST_COST"] |
| fallbackOn | 数组：枚举 | 否 | uniqueItems: true；省略允许四种瞬时错误（含 MODEL_CIRCUIT_OPEN），空数组关闭故障切换。已开始文字或工具参数输出时始终禁止切换。 |
| maxAttempts | integer | 否 | minimum: 1；maximum: 8；默认候选数，不能超过实际候选数。 |
| attemptTimeoutSeconds | integer | 否 | default: 60；minimum: 1；maximum: 60 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "strategy": {
      "enum": [
        "ORDERED",
        "LOWEST_COST"
      ],
      "default": "ORDERED"
    },
    "fallbackOn": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "enum": [
          "MODEL_UNAVAILABLE",
          "MODEL_TIMED_OUT",
          "MODEL_RATE_LIMITED",
          "MODEL_CIRCUIT_OPEN"
        ]
      },
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "description": "省略允许四种瞬时错误（含 MODEL_CIRCUIT_OPEN），空数组关闭故障切换。已开始文字或工具参数输出时始终禁止切换。"
    },
    "maxAttempts": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 8,
      "description": "默认候选数，不能超过实际候选数。"
    },
    "attemptTimeoutSeconds": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 60,
      "default": 60
    }
  }
}
```

:::

## ModelAccountingSummary {#schema-modelaccountingsummary}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| coverage | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["COMPLETE","LEGACY"]；COMPLETE 表示该 Run 启用账本；LEGACY 不补造历史调用。 |
| invocations | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000 |
| sent | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000 |
| notSent | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000 |
| unknownCostInvocations | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000 |
| providerUsage | [ModelProviderUsage](./数据结构.md#schema-modelproviderusage) | 是 | — |
| cost | object / null | 是 | 不允许额外字段 |
| cost.source | string | 是 | const: "configured-pricing" |
| cost.amounts | object | 是 | — |
| knownConfiguredAmounts | object | 是 | 按币种累计十进制金额，不自动换算。 |
| providerBilledAmounts | object | 是 | 按币种累计十进制金额，不自动换算。 |
| billedComplete | boolean | 是 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "coverage": {
      "enum": [
        "COMPLETE",
        "LEGACY"
      ],
      "description": "COMPLETE 表示该 Run 启用账本；LEGACY 不补造历史调用。"
    },
    "invocations": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1000000
    },
    "sent": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1000000
    },
    "notSent": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1000000
    },
    "unknownCostInvocations": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1000000
    },
    "providerUsage": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ModelProviderUsage"
    },
    "cost": {
      "type": [
        "object",
        "null"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "source": {
          "const": "configured-pricing"
        },
        "amounts": {
          "type": "object",
          "additionalProperties": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "source",
        "amounts"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "knownConfiguredAmounts": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "string"
      },
      "description": "按币种累计十进制金额，不自动换算。"
    },
    "providerBilledAmounts": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "string"
      },
      "description": "按币种累计十进制金额，不自动换算。"
    },
    "billedComplete": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "coverage",
    "invocations",
    "sent",
    "notSent",
    "unknownCostInvocations",
    "providerUsage",
    "cost",
    "knownConfiguredAmounts",
    "providerBilledAmounts",
    "billedComplete"
  ]
}
```

## ModelInvocation {#schema-modelinvocation}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | — |
| invocationId | string | 是 | — |
| runId | string | 是 | — |
| appId | string | 是 | — |
| subjectId | string | 是 | — |
| tenantId | string | 是 | — |
| stepId | string | 是 | — |
| agentCallId | string | 是 | — |
| parentAgentCallId | string / null | 是 | — |
| agentId | string | 是 | — |
| depth | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 8 |
| attempt | integer | 是 | minimum: 1；maximum: 8 |
| binding | [Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 是 | — |
| model | object | 是 | 不允许额外字段 |
| model.id | string | 是 | — |
| model.version | string | 是 | — |
| model.scope | string | 是 | — |

| provider.version | string | 是 | — |
| provider.scope | string | 是 | — |
| selectionReason | string | 是 | — |
| status | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["RUNNING","RESPONDED","FAILED","SKIPPED","UNKNOWN"] |
| sendState | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["NOT_SENT","SENT"]；SENT 表示进入发送边界，可能被接收；不证明供应商收到。 |
| outputStarted | boolean | 是 | — |
| pricing | object | 是 | 当次固定模型价目，未配置时为空对象。 |

| pricingHash | string | 是 | — |

| cost.amounts | object | 是 | — |
| createdAt | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 9223372036854776000 |
| sentAt | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 9223372036854776000 |
| receiptAt | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 9223372036854776000 |
| finishedAt | integer / null | 否 | — |
| revision | integer | 是 | minimum: 1；maximum: 2147483647 |
| errorCode | string / null | 否 | — |
| effectiveUsage | [ModelProviderUsage](./数据结构.md#schema-modelproviderusage) | 是 | — |
| effectiveCost | object / null | 是 | 不允许额外字段 |
| effectiveCost.source | string | 是 | const: "configured-pricing" |
| effectiveCost.amounts | object | 是 | — |
| costDifference | object / null | 是 | 仅可比币种有完整配置金额时返回账单减配置费用。 |
| costDifference.currency | string | 否 | — |
| costDifference.amount | string | 否 | — |
| reconciliation | [ModelReconciliation](./数据结构.md#schema-modelreconciliation) | 否 | — |
| reconciliationHistory | 数组：[ModelReconciliation](./数据结构.md#schema-modelreconciliation) | 否 | maxItems: 100；明细接口返回；列表不返回历史数组。 |
| effectiveUsageSource | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["NOT_SENT","UNKNOWN","PROVIDER","PROVIDER_RECONCILIATION"] |
| agentVersion | string | 否 | pattern: "^(0\|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0\|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0\|[1-9][0-9]*)$" |

```json
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9223372036854776000
    },
    "errorCode": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "priorHealthState": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "healthId",
    "appId",
    "binding",
    "model",
    "provider",
    "createdAt",
    "status",
    "generation",
    "checks",
    "reason",
    "requestedBy",
    "maxOutputTokensPerCheck",
    "pricing",
    "pricingVersion",
    "evidence"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## ModelProbeRequest {#schema-modelproberequest}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| checks | 数组：枚举 | 是 | minItems: 1；maxItems: 4；uniqueItems: true；必须包含 TEXT；固定合成提示词，不执行业务工具。 |
| reason | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 512 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "checks": {
      "type": "array",
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 4,
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "items": {
        "enum": [
          "TEXT",
          "TOOL_CALLING",
          "STREAMING",
          "STRUCTURED_OUTPUT"
        ]
      },
      "description": "必须包含 TEXT；固定合成提示词，不执行业务工具。"
    },
    "reason": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 512
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "checks",
    "reason"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## RemoteModelToolCall {#schema-remotemodeltoolcall}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128；pattern: "\\S" |
| type | string | 是 | const: "function" |
| function | object | 是 | 不允许额外字段 |
| function.name | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128；pattern: "\\S" |
| function.arguments | string | 是 | 完整闭合 JSON 对象，拒绝重复属性、尾随 JSON 与 null |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128,
      "pattern": "\\S"
    },
    "type": {
      "const": "function"
    },
    "function": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "name": {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 128,
          "pattern": "\\S"
        },
        "arguments": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "完整闭合 JSON 对象，拒绝重复属性、尾随 JSON 与 null"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "name",
        "arguments"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "type",
    "function"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## RemoteModelMessage {#schema-remotemodelmessage}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| role | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["system","user","assistant","tool"] |
| content | string / null | 否 | — |
| tool_calls | 数组：[RemoteModelToolCall](./数据结构.md#schema-remotemodeltoolcall) | 否 | maxItems: 16 |
| tool_call_id | string | 否 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128；pattern: "\\S" |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "role": {
      "enum": [
        "system",
        "user",
        "assistant",
        "tool"
      ]
    },
    "content": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "tool_calls": {
      "type": "array",
      "maxItems": 16,
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/RemoteModelToolCall"
      }
    },
    "tool_call_id": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128,
      "pattern": "\\S"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "role"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "allOf": [
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "role": {
            "const": "tool"
          }
        }
      },
      "then": {
        "required": [
          "tool_call_id"
        ]
      },
      "else": {
        "not": {
          "required": [
            "tool_call_id"
          ]
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "role": {
            "const": "assistant"
          }
        }
      },
      "then": {
        "anyOf": [
          {
            "required": [
              "content"
            ],
            "properties": {
              "content": {
                "type": "string"
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "required": [
              "tool_calls"
            ],
            "properties": {
              "tool_calls": {
                "minItems": 1
              }
            }
          }
        ]
      },
      "else": {
        "required": [
          "content"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "content": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "not": {
          "required": [
            "tool_calls"
          ]
        }
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

:::

## RemoteModelRequest {#schema-remotemodelrequest}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| protocolVersion | string | 是 | const: "1.0" |
| invocationId | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 256；pattern: "\\S" |
| deadline | integer | 是 | minimum: 1；maximum: 9223372036854776000；绝对 UTC 毫秒；回调最多等待 60 秒 |
| purpose | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["BUSINESS","SYNTHETIC_PROBE"] |
| appId | string | 是 | pattern: "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$" |
| subjectId | string | 否 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 256；pattern: "\\S" |
| tenantId | string | 否 | — |
| runId | string | 否 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 256；pattern: "\\S" |
| traceId | string | 否 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 256；pattern: "\\S" |
| model | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128；pattern: "\\S" |
| messages | 数组：[RemoteModelMessage](./数据结构.md#schema-remotemodelmessage) | 是 | minItems: 1；maxItems: 256 |
| tools | 数组：object | 否 | maxItems: 128 |
| max_completion_tokens | integer | 是 | minimum: 1；maximum: 10000000 |
| stream | boolean | 是 | — |
| response_format | object | 否 | — |
| tool_choice | 对象 | 否 | 合成探测或受控模型请求中的工具选择 |
| temperature | number | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 2 |
| top_p | number | 否 | maximum: 1；exclusiveMinimum: 0 |
| seed | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 9223372036854776000 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "protocolVersion": {
      "const": "1.0"
    },
    "invocationId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "pattern": "\\S"
    },
    "deadline": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 9223372036854776000,
      "description": "绝对 UTC 毫秒；回调最多等待 60 秒"
    },
    "purpose": {
      "enum": [
        "BUSINESS",
        "SYNTHETIC_PROBE"
      ]
    },
    "appId": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$"
    },
    "subjectId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "pattern": "\\S"
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "runId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "pattern": "\\S"
    },
    "traceId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "pattern": "\\S"
    },
    "model": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128,
      "pattern": "\\S"
    },
    "messages": {
      "type": "array",
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 256,
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/RemoteModelMessage"
      }
    },
    "tools": {
      "type": "array",
      "maxItems": 128,
      "items": {
        "type": "object"
      }
    },
    "max_completion_tokens": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 10000000
    },
    "stream": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "response_format": {
      "type": "object"
    },
    "tool_choice": {
      "description": "合成探测或受控模型请求中的工具选择"
    },
    "temperature": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 2
    },
    "top_p": {
      "type": "number",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1
    },
    "seed": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9223372036854776000
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "protocolVersion",
    "invocationId",
    "deadline",
    "purpose",
    "appId",
    "model",
    "messages",
    "max_completion_tokens",
    "stream"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "allOf": [
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "purpose": {
            "const": "BUSINESS"
          }
        }
      },
      "then": {
        "required": [
          "subjectId",
          "tenantId",
          "runId",
          "traceId"
        ]
      },
      "else": {
        "not": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "required": [
                "subjectId"
              ]
            },
            {
              "required": [
                "tenantId"
              ]
            },
            {
              "required": [
                "runId"
              ]
            },
            {
              "required": [
                "traceId"
              ]
            }
          ]
        }
      }
    }
  ],
  "description": "REMOTE 1.0 回调；平台出站认证，SDK 独立凭据与消费应用白名单验证。JSON 正文最大 1 MiB；身份来自平台授权。"
}
```

:::

## RemoteModelStreamFrame {#schema-remotemodelstreamframe}



类型：[RemoteModelTextDelta](./数据结构.md#schema-remotemodeltextdelta) / [RemoteModelToolDelta](./数据结构.md#schema-remotemodeltooldelta) / [RemoteModelUsageDelta](./数据结构.md#schema-remotemodelusagedelta) / [RemoteModelCompleted](./数据结构.md#schema-remotemodelcompleted) / [RemoteModelError](./数据结构.md#schema-remotemodelerror)。每个 JSON 对象为一条 data: SSE 帧；必须明确终结，拒绝 [DONE]、未知帧/字段、重复终结和截断。最大 4 MiB；工具参数拼接后必须是闭合 JSON 对象。

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "oneOf": [
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/RemoteModelTextDelta"
    },
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/RemoteModelToolDelta"
    },
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/RemoteModelUsageDelta"
    },
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/RemoteModelCompleted"
    },
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/RemoteModelError"
    }
  ],
  "description": "每个 JSON 对象为一条 data: SSE 帧；必须明确终结，拒绝 [DONE]、未知帧/字段、重复终结和截断。最大 4 MiB；工具参数拼接后必须是闭合 JSON 对象。"
}
```

:::

## RemoteModelFailure {#schema-remotemodelfailure}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | object | 是 | 不允许额外字段 |
| error.code | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["MODEL_UNAVAILABLE","MODEL_TIMED_OUT","MODEL_RATE_LIMITED","MODEL_AUTH_FAILED","MODEL_REQUEST_REJECTED","MODEL_FEATURE_UNSUPPORTED","MODEL_PROTOCOL_ERROR"] |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "error": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "code": {
          "enum": [
            "MODEL_UNAVAILABLE",
            "MODEL_TIMED_OUT",
            "MODEL_RATE_LIMITED",
            "MODEL_AUTH_FAILED",
            "MODEL_REQUEST_REJECTED",
            "MODEL_FEATURE_UNSUPPORTED",
            "MODEL_PROTOCOL_ERROR"
          ]
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "code"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "error"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "description": "REMOTE 非 2xx 的受控错误合同；平台优先读取代码，错误正文不进入用户事件。协议/认证错误不因 HTTP 5xx 变成可重试故障。"
}
```

:::

## QuotaPolicy {#schema-quotapolicy}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | pattern: "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$" |
| scope | string | 是 | enum: ["APP","TENANT","USER","MODEL"] |
| value | string | 否 | default: "*"；minLength: 1；maxLength: 256；USER 通配按可信 tenantId/subjectId 分桶；MODEL 为 scope/models/id@version 或 * |
| period | string | 是 | enum: ["MINUTE","HOUR","DAY","MONTH"]；UTC 固定窗口，多条规则同时生效 |
| enabled | boolean | 否 | default: true |
| maxAmount | string | 否 | pattern: "^[0-9]{1,13}(\\.[0-9]{1,12})?$" |
| currency | string | 否 | pattern: "^[A-Z]{3}$" |
| unknownPriceMode | string | 否 | enum: ["REJECT","SEPARATE"]；金额规则必须 REJECT；SEPARATE 必须另设未知价请求额度，不能带 maxAmount |
| maxConcurrentRuns | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000000000 |
| maxRunRequests | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000000000 |
| maxConcurrentModels | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000000000 |
| maxModelRequests | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000000000 |
| maxTokens | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000000000 |
| maxUnpricedRequests | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1000000000000 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$"
    },
    "scope": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "APP",
        "TENANT",
        "USER",
        "MODEL"
      ]
    },
    "value": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256,
      "default": "*",
      "description": "USER 通配按可信 tenantId/subjectId 分桶；MODEL 为 scope/models/id@version 或 *"
    },
    "period": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "MINUTE",
        "HOUR",
        "DAY",
        "MONTH"
      ],
      "description": "UTC 固定窗口，多条规则同时生效"
    },
    "enabled": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "maxAmount": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{1,13}(\\.[0-9]{1,12})?$"
    },
    "currency": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[A-Z]{3}$"
    },
    "unknownPriceMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "REJECT",
        "SEPARATE"
      ],
      "description": "金额规则必须 REJECT；SEPARATE 必须另设未知价请求额度，不能带 maxAmount"
    },
    "maxConcurrentRuns": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1000000000000
    },
    "maxRunRequests": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1000000000000
    },
    "maxConcurrentModels": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1000000000000
    },
    "maxModelRequests": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1000000000000
    },
    "maxTokens": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1000000000000
    },
    "maxUnpricedRequests": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1000000000000
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "scope",
    "period"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "description": "至少一个上限；零代表禁止。MODEL 不接受 Run 上限，币种须与模型价目一致。已有策略范围/周期/币种不可修改。token/金额预留要求 capabilities.contextWindow，计量以完整终结回执或对账为准。"
}
```

:::

```json
      },
      "maxItems": 64
    },

      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024
    }
  },
  "required": [

    "reason"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

```json
      "minimum": 0
    },

      "minimum": 0,

    },
```

```json
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9000000000000000
    },
    "id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "appId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "policyId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "scopeKey": {
      "type": "string"
    },

      "type": "string"
    },

      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "MINUTE",
        "HOUR",
        "DAY",
        "MONTH",
        "RUN"
      ]
    },
    "subjectId": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },

      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "currency": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "heldAmount": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{1,13}(\\.[0-9]{1,12})?$"
```

## QuotaReservation {#schema-quotareservation}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | — |
| appId | string | 是 | — |
| runId | string | 是 | — |
| model | string | 是 | — |
| probeId | string | 是 | — |
| probeCheck | string | 是 | — |
| purpose | string | 是 | enum: ["BUSINESS","SYNTHETIC_PROBE"] |
| status | string | 是 | enum: ["HELD","UNKNOWN","SETTLED"] |
| subjectId | string / null | 是 | — |
| tenantId | string / null | 是 | — |
| createdAt | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 9000000000000000 |
| deadline | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 9000000000000000 |
| finishedAt | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 9000000000000000 |
| configRevision | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 9000000000000000 |
| revision | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 9000000000000000 |
| pricing | object | 是 | 固定发布模型价目；未知价为空对象 |
| parts | 数组：[QuotaReservationPart](./数据结构.md#schema-quotareservationpart) | 是 | — |
| providerUsage | [ModelProviderUsage](./数据结构.md#schema-modelproviderusage) | 否 | — |
| billedCost | object / null | 否 | 不允许额外字段 |
| billedCost.currency | string | 否 | — |
| billedCost.amount | string | 否 | pattern: "^[0-9]{1,13}(\\.[0-9]{1,12})?$" |
| usageSource | string | 否 | — |
| reconciliationHistory | 数组：object | 否 | maxItems: 100 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "appId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "runId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "model": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "probeId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "probeCheck": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "purpose": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "BUSINESS",
        "SYNTHETIC_PROBE"
      ]
    },
    "status": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "HELD",
        "UNKNOWN",
        "SETTLED"
      ]
    },
    "subjectId": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "createdAt": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9000000000000000
    },
    "deadline": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9000000000000000
    },
    "finishedAt": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9000000000000000
    },
    "configRevision": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9000000000000000
    },
    "revision": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9000000000000000
    },
    "pricing": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "固定发布模型价目；未知价为空对象"
    },
    "parts": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/QuotaReservationPart"
      }
    },
    "providerUsage": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ModelProviderUsage"
    },
    "billedCost": {
      "type": [
        "object",
        "null"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "currency": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "amount": {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^[0-9]{1,13}(\\.[0-9]{1,12})?$"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "usageSource": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "reconciliationHistory": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object"
      },
      "maxItems": 100
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "appId",
    "runId",
    "model",
    "purpose",
    "status",
    "createdAt",
    "deadline",
    "configRevision",
    "revision",
    "pricing",
    "parts",
    "probeId",
    "probeCheck",
    "subjectId",
    "tenantId"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "description": "BUSINESS 在模型调用账本对账；合成探测通过预留对账，仅修正预算事实，不修改探测原回执或健康结论。"
}
```

:::

```json
{
  "type": "object",
```

```json
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "depth": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
```
