```json
    "agentVersion": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$"
    },
    "status": {
      "const": "SUCCEEDED"
    },
    "text": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "output": {
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "type": {
              "const": "TEXT"
            },
            "value": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "type",
            "value"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "type": {
              "const": "JSON"
            },
            "value": {
              "type": "object",
              "additionalProperties": true
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "type",
            "value"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ]
    },
    "usage": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AgentBudgetUsage"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "agentCallId",
    "parentAgentCallId",
    "depth",
    "agentId",
    "agentVersion",
    "status",
    "text",
    "output",
    "usage"
  ]
}
```

:::

## KnowledgeFilter {#schema-knowledgefilter}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| appId | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 64；pattern: "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$" |
| subjectId | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128 |
| tenantId | string | 是 | minLength: 0；maxLength: 128 |
| required | 数组：枚举 | 是 | maxItems: 2；uniqueItems: true |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "appId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64,
      "pattern": "^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$"
    },
    "subjectId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 0,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "required": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "enum": [
          "TENANT",
          "SUBJECT"
        ]
      },
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "maxItems": 2
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "appId",
    "subjectId",
    "tenantId",
    "required"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## KnowledgeSource {#schema-knowledgesource}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["DOCUMENT","URL","INLINE"] |
| id | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128 |
| uri | string | 否 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 2048；pattern: "^https?://" |
| version | string | 否 | minLength: 0；maxLength: 128 |
| page | integer | 否 | minimum: 1；maximum: 1000000 |
| locator | string | 否 | minLength: 0；maxLength: 512 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "type": {
      "enum": [
        "DOCUMENT",
        "URL",
        "INLINE"
      ]
    },
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "uri": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 2048,
      "pattern": "^https?://"
    },
    "version": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 0,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "page": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 1000000
    },
    "locator": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 0,
      "maxLength": 512
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "type",
    "id"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "allOf": [
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "type": {
            "const": "URL"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "type"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "required": [
          "uri"
        ]
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

:::

## KnowledgeItem {#schema-knowledgeitem}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128；pattern: "^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,128}$" |
| documentId | string | 否 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128；pattern: "^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,128}$" |
| content | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 16384 |
| title | string | 否 | minLength: 0；maxLength: 512 |
| source | [KnowledgeSource](./数据结构.md#schema-knowledgesource) | 是 | — |
| score | number | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 1 |
| metadata | object | 否 | 最多 4096 UTF-8 字节、嵌套深度 8；有限 JSON 值。 |
| tenantId | string | 否 | minLength: 0；maxLength: 128 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128,
      "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,128}$"
    },
    "documentId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128,
      "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,128}$"
    },
    "content": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 16384
    },
    "title": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 0,
      "maxLength": 512
    },
    "source": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/KnowledgeSource"
    },
    "score": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1
    },
    "metadata": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": true,
      "description": "最多 4096 UTF-8 字节、嵌套深度 8；有限 JSON 值。"
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 0,
      "maxLength": 128
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "content",
    "source"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## KnowledgeCallbackRequest {#schema-knowledgecallbackrequest}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| protocolVersion | string | 是 | const: "1.0" |
| appId | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 64 |
| subjectId | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128 |
| tenantId | string | 是 | minLength: 0；maxLength: 128 |
| knowledgeId | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 64 |
| version | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 64 |
| query | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 16384 |
| topK | integer | 是 | minimum: 1；maximum: 10 |
| filters | [KnowledgeFilter](./数据结构.md#schema-knowledgefilter) | 是 | — |
| runId | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128 |
| traceId | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128 |
| executionId | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128 |
| deadline | integer | 是 | minimum: 1；maximum: 9223372036854776000 |
| trustedContext | object | 是 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "protocolVersion": {
      "const": "1.0"
    },
    "appId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64
    },
    "subjectId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 0,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "knowledgeId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64
    },
    "version": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64
    },
    "query": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 16384
    },
    "topK": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 10
    },
    "filters": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/KnowledgeFilter"
    },
    "runId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "traceId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "executionId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "deadline": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 9223372036854776000
    },
    "trustedContext": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": true
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "protocolVersion",
    "appId",
    "subjectId",
    "tenantId",
    "knowledgeId",
    "version",
    "query",
    "topK",
    "filters",
    "runId",
    "traceId",
    "executionId",
    "deadline",
    "trustedContext"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## KnowledgeCallbackResult {#schema-knowledgecallbackresult}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| protocolVersion | string | 是 | const: "1.0" |
| items | 数组：[KnowledgeItem](./数据结构.md#schema-knowledgeitem) | 是 | maxItems: 100 |
| appliedFilters | 数组：枚举 | 是 | maxItems: 2；uniqueItems: true |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "protocolVersion": {
      "const": "1.0"
    },
    "items": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/KnowledgeItem"
      },
      "maxItems": 100
    },
    "appliedFilters": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "enum": [
          "TENANT",
          "SUBJECT"
        ]
      },
      "uniqueItems": true,
      "maxItems": 2
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "protocolVersion",
    "items",
    "appliedFilters"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "description": "完整结果上限 1 MiB；id 不重复。空结果正常无匹配；必须确认定义要求的全部过滤。"
}
```

:::

## KnowledgeCallbackError {#schema-knowledgecallbackerror}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | object | 是 | 不允许额外字段 |
| error.code | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["KNOWLEDGE_ACCESS_DENIED","KNOWLEDGE_TIMED_OUT","KNOWLEDGE_UNAVAILABLE","KNOWLEDGE_RATE_LIMITED","KNOWLEDGE_REQUEST_REJECTED","KNOWLEDGE_PROTOCOL_ERROR"] |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "error": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "code": {
          "enum": [
            "KNOWLEDGE_ACCESS_DENIED",
            "KNOWLEDGE_TIMED_OUT",
            "KNOWLEDGE_UNAVAILABLE",
            "KNOWLEDGE_RATE_LIMITED",
            "KNOWLEDGE_REQUEST_REJECTED",
            "KNOWLEDGE_PROTOCOL_ERROR"
          ]
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "code"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "error"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## KnowledgeRouteRule {#schema-knowledgerouterule}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| knowledge | [Ref](./数据结构.md#schema-ref) | 是 | — |
| queryTerms | 数组：string | 否 | maxItems: 16；uniqueItems: true |
| tags | 数组：string | 否 | maxItems: 16；uniqueItems: true |
| contextEquals | object | 否 | — |
| priority | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 100 |
| always | boolean | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "knowledge": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Ref"
    },
    "queryTerms": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 64
      },
      "maxItems": 16,
      "uniqueItems": true
    },
    "tags": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 64
      },
      "maxItems": 16,
      "uniqueItems": true
    },
    "contextEquals": {
      "type": "object",
      "maxProperties": 16,
      "propertyNames": {
        "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]{0,63}$"
      },
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 0,
        "maxLength": 128
      }
    },
    "priority": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100
    },
    "always": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "knowledge"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "description": "仅引用当前 Agent.allowedKnowledge 精确版本；条件需要 always=true、词项/标签或可信上下文。上下文与词项条件同时配置时共同匹配。规则来源不能重复。"
}
```

:::

## KnowledgeRoutingPolicy {#schema-knowledgeroutingpolicy}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| mode | 枚举 | 否 | default: "AUTO"；enum: ["EAGER","AUTO","ON_DEMAND"] |
| failurePolicy | 枚举 | 否 | default: "FAIL_FAST"；enum: ["FAIL_FAST","SKIP_TRANSIENT"] |
| rerank | 枚举 | 否 | default: "NONE"；enum: ["NONE","LEXICAL"] |
| maxSources | integer | 否 | minimum: 1；maximum: 16 |
| topK | integer | 否 | minimum: 1；maximum: 50 |
| maxRetrievals | integer | 否 | minimum: 1；maximum: 64 |
| maxQueriesPerSource | integer | 否 | minimum: 1；maximum: 16 |
| sourceTimeoutSeconds | integer | 否 | minimum: 1；maximum: 60 |
| sourceMaxChars | integer | 否 | minimum: 1；maximum: 65536 |
| maxContextChars | integer | 否 | minimum: 1；maximum: 65536 |
| rules | 数组：[KnowledgeRouteRule](./数据结构.md#schema-knowledgerouterule) | 否 | maxItems: 64 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "mode": {
      "enum": [
        "EAGER",
        "AUTO",
        "ON_DEMAND"
      ],
      "default": "AUTO"
    },
    "failurePolicy": {
      "enum": [
        "FAIL_FAST",
        "SKIP_TRANSIENT"
      ],
      "default": "FAIL_FAST"
    },
    "rerank": {
      "enum": [
        "NONE",
        "LEXICAL"
      ],
      "default": "NONE"
    },
    "maxSources": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 16
    },
    "topK": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 50
    },
    "maxRetrievals": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 64
    },
    "maxQueriesPerSource": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 16
    },
    "sourceTimeoutSeconds": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 60
    },
    "sourceMaxChars": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 65536
    },
    "maxContextChars": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 65536
    },
    "rules": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/KnowledgeRouteRule"
      },
      "maxItems": 64
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## KnowledgeSelectedSource {#schema-knowledgeselectedsource}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 64 |
| version | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 64 |
| reason | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["MODEL_REQUEST","EAGER","RULE_ALWAYS","TRUSTED_CONTEXT","QUERY_MATCH","TAG_MATCH","DESCRIPTION_TAG_MATCH"] |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64
    },
    "version": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64
    },
    "reason": {
      "enum": [
        "MODEL_REQUEST",
        "EAGER",
        "RULE_ALWAYS",
        "TRUSTED_CONTEXT",
        "QUERY_MATCH",
        "TAG_MATCH",
        "DESCRIPTION_TAG_MATCH"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "version",
    "reason"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## DocumentChunkingPolicy {#schema-documentchunkingpolicy}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| strategy | 枚举 | 否 | default: "CHARACTER"；enum: ["CHARACTER","PARAGRAPH","MARKDOWN"] |
| maxChars | integer | 否 | default: 2000；minimum: 128；maximum: 8192 |
| overlapChars | integer | 否 | default: 0；minimum: 0；maximum: 4096 |
| preserveParagraphs | boolean | 否 | — |
| preserveCodeBlocks | boolean | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "strategy": {
      "enum": [
        "CHARACTER",
        "PARAGRAPH",
        "MARKDOWN"
      ],
      "default": "CHARACTER"
    },
    "maxChars": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 128,
      "maximum": 8192,
      "default": 2000
    },
    "overlapChars": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 4096,
      "default": 0
    },
    "preserveParagraphs": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "preserveCodeBlocks": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "description": "仅 MANAGED；overlapChars <= maxChars/2，非 CHARACTER 必须为 0。段落保护默认非 CHARACTER 启用；代码保护默认仅 MARKDOWN 启用。CHARACTER 不允许段落保护，非 MARKDOWN 不允许代码保护。缺省 chunking 保留旧 2000 字符策略。"
}
```

:::

```json
      "type": "string",

    },

      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 0,

    },

      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 0,
      "maxLength": 128
    }

  "additionalProperties": false
```

## ManagedDocumentImport {#schema-manageddocumentimport}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | pattern: "^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{1,128}$" |
| text | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 65536 |
| title | string | 否 | minLength: 0；maxLength: 512 |
| source | string | 否 | minLength: 0；maxLength: 2048 |
| tenantId | string | 否 | minLength: 0；maxLength: 128 |
| duplicatePolicy | 枚举 | 否 | default: "UPSERT"；enum: ["UPSERT","SKIP","REJECT"] |
| expectedDocumentVersion | string | 否 | pattern: "^[a-f0-9]{64}$" |

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{1,128}$",
      "not": {
        "enum": [
          ".",
          ".."
        ]
      }
    },
    "text": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 65536
    },
    "title": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 0,
      "maxLength": 512
    },
    "source": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 0,
      "maxLength": 2048
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 0,
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "duplicatePolicy": {
      "enum": [
        "UPSERT",
        "SKIP",
        "REJECT"
      ],
      "default": "UPSERT"
    },

      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-f0-9]{64}$"
    }

  "required": [

  ],

}
```

## ManagedDocumentResult {#schema-manageddocumentresult}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | pattern: "^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{1,128}$" |
| status | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["INDEXED","UNCHANGED","SKIPPED"] |
| chunkCount | integer | 是 | minimum: 1；maximum: 512 |
| documentVersion | string | 是 | pattern: "^[a-f0-9]{64}$" |
| contentHash | string | 是 | pattern: "^[a-f0-9]{64}$" |
| chunkingHash | string | 是 | pattern: "^[a-f0-9]{64}$" |

## ManagedDocumentBatchRequest {#schema-manageddocumentbatchrequest}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| documents | 数组：[ManagedDocumentImport](./数据结构.md#schema-manageddocumentimport) | 是 | minItems: 1；maxItems: 25 |
| atomic | boolean | 否 | default: true |
| duplicatePolicy | 枚举 | 否 | default: "UPSERT"；enum: ["UPSERT","SKIP","REJECT"] |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "documents": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ManagedDocumentImport"
      },
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 25
    },
    "atomic": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "duplicatePolicy": {
      "enum": [
        "UPSERT",
        "SKIP",
        "REJECT"
      ],
      "default": "UPSERT"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "documents"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "description": "唯一文档 ID，正文总计 <= 1048576 UTF-16 字符；atomic 默认 true，失败有明细且零写入；false 每条独立事务。document 中 duplicatePolicy 可覆盖批次策略。"
}
```

:::

## ManagedDocumentRebuildRequest {#schema-manageddocumentrebuildrequest}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| sourceVersion | string | 是 | maxLength: 64；pattern: "^(0\|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0\|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0\|[1-9][0-9]*)$" |
| documentIds | 数组：string | 是 | minItems: 1；maxItems: 25；uniqueItems: true |
| atomic | boolean | 否 | default: true |
| duplicatePolicy | 枚举 | 否 | default: "UPSERT"；enum: ["UPSERT","SKIP","REJECT"] |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "sourceVersion": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$",
      "maxLength": 64
    },
    "documentIds": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{1,128}$",
        "not": {
          "enum": [
            ".",
            ".."
          ]
        }
      },
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 25,
      "uniqueItems": true
    },
    "atomic": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "duplicatePolicy": {
      "enum": [
        "UPSERT",
        "SKIP",
        "REJECT"
      ],
      "default": "UPSERT"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "sourceVersion",
    "documentIds"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "description": "源必须同一资源不同的 PUBLISHED MANAGED 版本，目标 DRAFT；逐个文档检查租户与 ACL；不返回正文，不自动发布。"
}
```

:::

```json
    "revision": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "attempt": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "createdAt": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "updatedAt": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "result": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ManagedDocumentResult"
    },
    "error": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "code": {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 128
        },
        "message": {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 256
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "code",
        "message"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "parsedDocumentVersion": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-f0-9]{64}$",
      "description": "Canonical parsed candidate version; SKIPPED result may retain a different indexed version"
    },
    "parsedContentHash": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-f0-9]{64}$"
    },
    "pageCount": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 100
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "appId",
    "knowledgeId",
    "knowledgeVersion",
    "documentId",
    "filename",
    "format",
    "sizeBytes",
    "originalHash",
    "title",
    "source",
    "tenantId",
    "duplicatePolicy",
    "status",
    "revision",
    "attempt",
    "createdAt",
    "updatedAt"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

```json
    },
```

```json
      ]
```

## KnowledgeDebugHit {#schema-knowledgedebughit}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | maxLength: 160 |
| documentId | string | 是 | pattern: "^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{1,128}$" |
| title | string | 否 | maxLength: 512 |
| source | string | 否 | maxLength: 2048 |
| tenantId | string | 否 | maxLength: 128 |
| text | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 8192 |
| startOffset | integer | 否 | minimum: 0；maximum: 65536 |
| endOffset | integer | 否 | minimum: 1；maximum: 65536 |
| documentVersion | string | 否 | pattern: "^[a-f0-9]{64}$" |
| sourceRef | [KnowledgeSource](./数据结构.md#schema-knowledgesource) | 否 | — |
| rank | integer | 是 | minimum: 1；maximum: 20 |
| score | number | 是 | minimum: -1 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 160
    },
    "documentId": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{1,128}$",
      "not": {
        "enum": [
          ".",
          ".."
        ]
      }
    },
    "title": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 512
    },
    "source": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 2048
    },
    "tenantId": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 128
    },
    "text": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 8192,
      "minLength": 1
    },
    "startOffset": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 65536
    },
    "endOffset": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 65536
    },
    "documentVersion": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-f0-9]{64}$"
    },
    "sourceRef": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/KnowledgeSource"
    },
    "rank": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 20
    },
    "score": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -1
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "documentId",
    "text",
    "rank",
    "score"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "description": "Old stored chunks may omit source metadata and UTF-16 offsets; the platform supplies parent document identity but does not invent provenance."
}
```

:::

## KnowledgeEvaluationRequest {#schema-knowledgeevaluationrequest}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| name | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 128 |
| versions | 数组：string | 是 | minItems: 1；maxItems: 2；uniqueItems: true |
| topK | integer | 否 | default: 5；minimum: 1；maximum: 20 |

```json

  "properties": {

      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 128,
      "minLength": 1
    },

      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "maxLength": 64,
        "pattern": "^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$"
      },
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 2,
      "uniqueItems": true
    },
    "topK": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 20,
      "default": 5
    },
    "cases": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/KnowledgeEvaluationInputCase"
      },
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 10
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "name",
    "versions",
    "cases"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

```json
      "minItems": 1,

    },

      },
```
```json
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 2
```

```json
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "CURRENT",
        "STALE"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id",
    "name",
    "algorithm",
    "topK",
    "versions",
    "createdAt",
    "revision",
    "elapsedMs",
    "appId",
    "knowledgeId",
    "subjectId",
    "tenantId",
    "inputCases",
    "indexes",
    "results",
    "status"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## KnowledgeRetrieval {#schema-knowledgeretrieval}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| strategy | 枚举 | 是 | enum: ["LEXICAL","VECTOR","HYBRID"] |
| embedding | [EmbeddingConfiguration](./数据结构.md#schema-embeddingconfiguration) | 否 | — |
| minScore | number | 否 | default: 0；minimum: -1；maximum: 1 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "strategy": {
      "enum": [
        "LEXICAL",
        "VECTOR",
        "HYBRID"
      ]
    },
    "embedding": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/EmbeddingConfiguration"
    },
    "minScore": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -1,
      "maximum": 1,
      "default": 0
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "strategy"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "allOf": [
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "strategy": {
            "const": "LEXICAL"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "strategy"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "not": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "required": [
                "embedding"
              ]
            },
            {
              "required": [
                "minScore"
              ]
            }
          ]
        }
      },
      "else": {
        "required": [
          "embedding"
        ]
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

:::

```json
    "nextCursor": {
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 2048,
          "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "snapshot": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AuditSnapshot"
    },
    "total": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "limit": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 1000
    },
    "formatVersion": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "1.0"
      ]
    },
    "sha256": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-f0-9]{64}$"
    },
    "exportedAt": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "redacted": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "redactionRevision": {
      "type": "integer"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "items",
    "nextCursor",
    "snapshot",
    "total",
    "limit",
    "formatVersion",
    "sha256",
    "exportedAt"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "description": "At most 1000 records and 2 MiB of record bodies per file. sha256 is canonical JSON record digest, not a tamper-proof signature. nextCursor traverses the fixed snapshot."
}
```

:::

## AuditRetentionPlan {#schema-auditretentionplan}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| before | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；format: "int64" |
| limit | integer | 是 | default: 100；minimum: 1；maximum: 500 |
| snapshotPosition | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；format: "int64" |
| epoch | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；format: "int64" |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "before": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "limit": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 500,
      "default": 100
    },
    "snapshotPosition": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "epoch": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64",
      "minimum": 0
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "before",
    "limit",
    "snapshotPosition",
    "epoch"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## AuditRetentionPreviewRequest {#schema-auditretentionpreviewrequest}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| before | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；format: "int64" |
| limit | integer | 否 | default: 100；minimum: 1；maximum: 500 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "before": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "limit": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 500,
      "default": 100
    }
```

## ObservationSpan {#schema-observationspan}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| traceId | string | 是 | pattern: "^[0-9a-f]{32}$" |
| spanId | string | 是 | pattern: "^[0-9a-f]{16}$" |
| parentSpanId | string | 是 | 根 HTTP 上游可为空；对应节点可能只在外部 Collector 中。 |
| operation | string | 是 | enum: ["run","resolve","context","authorization","agent","subagent","model","tool","knowledge","confirmation"] |
| outcome | string | 是 | enum: ["OK","ERROR"] |
| startedAt | integer | 是 | — |
| finishedAt | integer | 是 | — |
| durationMs | number | 是 | minimum: 0 |
| attributes | object | 是 | 不允许额外字段 |
| attributes.runId | string | 是 | — |
| attributes.resourceKind | string | 否 | — |
| attributes.resourceId | string | 否 | — |
| attributes.resourceVersion | string | 否 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "description": "抽样元数据，无用户、租户、业务正文、参数及凭据；非完整审计。",
  "properties": {
    "traceId": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{32}$"
    },
    "spanId": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{16}$"
    },
    "parentSpanId": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "根 HTTP 上游可为空；对应节点可能只在外部 Collector 中。"
    },
    "operation": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "run",
        "resolve",
        "context",
        "authorization",
        "agent",
        "subagent",
        "model",
        "tool",
        "knowledge",
        "confirmation"
      ]
    },
    "outcome": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "OK",
        "ERROR"
      ]
    },
    "startedAt": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "finishedAt": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "durationMs": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "attributes": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "runId": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "resourceKind": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "resourceId": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "resourceVersion": {
          "type": "string"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "runId"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "traceId",
    "spanId",
    "parentSpanId",
    "operation",
    "outcome",
    "startedAt",
    "finishedAt",
    "durationMs",
    "attributes"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## RunTrace {#schema-runtrace}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| runId | string | 是 | — |
| traceId | string | 是 | — |
| recorded | boolean | 是 | — |
| sampled | boolean | 是 | — |
| rootRecorded | boolean | 是 | — |

```json
{

  "description": "异步保存；rootRecorded 仅表示终态 Span 已采集，不保证没有丢弃子节点。旧 Run、未抽样、到期或容量丢弃可能为空。",
  "properties": {
    "runId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "traceId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "recorded": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "sampled": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "rootRecorded": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "retentionHours": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "maxSpans": {
      "type": "integer",
      "const": 512
    },
    "items": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ObservationSpan"
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "runId",
    "traceId",
    "recorded",
    "sampled",
    "rootRecorded",
    "retentionHours",
    "maxSpans",
    "items"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## ObservationOperation {#schema-observationoperation}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| operation | string | 是 | — |
| outcome | string | 是 | enum: ["OK","ERROR"] |
| count | integer | 是 | minimum: 1 |
| averageMs | number | 是 | minimum: 0 |
| maximumMs | number | 是 | minimum: 0 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "description": "",
  "properties": {
    "operation": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "outcome": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "OK",
        "ERROR"
      ]
    },
    "count": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "averageMs": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "maximumMs": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "p95UpperBoundMs": {
      "type": [
        "number",
        "null"
      ],
      "description": "固定桶估算的 P95 上界；超过 600000ms 返回 null。"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "operation",
    "outcome",
    "count",
    "averageMs",
    "maximumMs",
    "p95UpperBoundMs"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## ObservationSummary {#schema-observationsummary}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| windowHours | integer | 是 | minimum: 1；maximum: 168 |
| from | integer | 是 | — |
| samplePercent | integer | 是 | minimum: 0；maximum: 100 |
| retentionHours | integer | 是 | — |
| basis | string | 是 | const: "RETAINED_SAMPLED_SPANS" |
| operations | 数组：[ObservationOperation](./数据结构.md#schema-observationoperation) | 是 | — |
| activeRuns | object | 是 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "description": "窗口基于保留的抽样 Span；不是完整业务请求总数。",
  "properties": {
    "windowHours": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 168
    },
    "from": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "samplePercent": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100
    },
    "retentionHours": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "basis": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "RETAINED_SAMPLED_SPANS"
    },
    "operations": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ObservationOperation"
      }
    },
    "activeRuns": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "integer",
        "minimum": 0
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "windowHours",
    "from",
    "samplePercent",
    "retentionHours",
    "basis",
    "operations",
    "activeRuns"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

```json
  "type": "object",
```

```json
        "UNKNOWN_WRITE",

        "HTTP_REJECTION",
        "BUDGET_EXHAUSTED"

      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 1000000
    },
    "windowSeconds": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 60,
      "maximum": 86400
    },

      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 60,
      "maximum": 86400

    },
```

```json
    "threshold": {

      "maximum": 86400

      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 256
    },
    "enabled": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "inhibitedBy": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "revision": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "createdAt": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "updatedAt": {
      "type": "integer"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

| autoSweep | [RetentionAutoSweep](./数据结构.md#schema-retentionautosweep) | 是 | — |
| lastSweepAt | integer | 是 | — |
| lastSweep | object | 是 | — |
| configuredCategories | 数组：string | 是 | — |
| counters | object | 是 | — |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "required": [
    "revision",
    "autoSweep",
    "lastSweepAt",
    "lastSweep",
    "configuredCategories",
    "counters"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "revision": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "autoSweep": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/RetentionAutoSweep"
    },
    "lastSweepAt": {
      "type": "integer"
    },

      "type": "object",

    },

      "type": "array",

      }

      }

}
```

## RedactionPattern {#schema-redactionpattern}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | string | 是 | pattern: "^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,63}$" |
| regex | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 256 |
| replacement | string | 是 | maxLength: 128 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "required": [
    "id",
    "regex",
    "replacement"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,63}$"
    },
    "regex": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 256
    },
    "replacement": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 128
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::

## RedactionPolicyInput {#schema-redactionpolicyinput}



| 字段 | 类型 | 本层必填 | 默认值与约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| enabled | boolean | 是 | — |
| mode | string | 是 | enum: ["TOKEN","HASH"] |
| token | string | 是 | maxLength: 32 |
| appliesTo | 数组：string | 是 | minItems: 1 |
| fields | 数组：string | 是 | maxItems: 64 |
| patterns | 数组：[RedactionPattern](./数据结构.md#schema-redactionpattern) | 是 | maxItems: 32 |
| reason | string | 是 | minLength: 1；maxLength: 512 |

::: details 完整机器定义

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "required": [
    "enabled",
    "mode",
    "token",
    "appliesTo",
    "fields",
    "patterns",
    "reason"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "enabled": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "mode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "TOKEN",
        "HASH"
      ]
    },
    "token": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 32
    },
    "appliesTo": {
      "type": "array",
      "minItems": 1,
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "enum": [
          "AUDIT",
          "ARCHIVES"
        ]
      }
    },
    "fields": {
      "type": "array",
      "maxItems": 64,
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,63}$"
      }
    },
    "patterns": {
      "type": "array",
      "maxItems": 32,
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/RedactionPattern"
      }
    },
    "reason": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 512
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

:::
